### 回答
以下是将你提供的C++代码翻译为Rust代码的实现，仅使用给定的库：

```rust
#![allow(dead_code)]
#![allow(unused_variables)]
#![allow(unused_imports)]
#![allow(unused_mut)]
#![allow(unused_assignments)]
#![cfg_attr(not(debug_assertions), windows_subsystem = "windows")]
#![allow(rustdoc::missing_crate_level_docs)]
#![allow(unsafe_code)]
#![allow(clippy::undocumented_unsafe_blocks)]
#![allow(unused_must_use)]
#![allow(non_snake_case)]

// 线性代数
use nalgebra::{
    DMatrix, DVector, Matrix3, Vector3, 
    Vector2, Matrix6, Vector6, Matrix2x6, 
    SMatrix, SVector
};

// 图像处理
use image::{
    open, ImageBuffer, Rgb, DynamicImage, Luma
};
use imageproc::{
    drawing::draw_cross_mut, drawing::draw_line_segment_mut
};

// ORB角点检测
use bye_orb_rs::{
    orb, fast, common::Matchable
};

// 图优化
use factrs::{
    assign_symbols,
    core::{BetweenResidual, GaussNewton, Graph, Values},
    dtype, fac,
    linalg::{Const, ForwardProp, Numeric, NumericalDiff, VectorX, DiffResult, MatrixX},
    residuals::Residual1,
    traits::*,
    variables::{VectorVar2, SE2, VectorVar3, SE3, SO2, SO3, MatrixLieGroup},
    containers::Key,
    noise::{GaussianNoise}
};

// 随机数
use rand::{
    Rng, SeedableRng
};
use rand::distributions::{
    Distribution, Uniform
};

// 时间
use std::time::Instant;

// 定义符号变量
assign_symbols!(X: VectorVar3);
assign_symbols!(Y: SE2);
assign_symbols!(Z: SE3);

// Camera intrinsics
const FX: f64 = 718.856;
const FY: f64 = 718.856;
const CX: f64 = 607.1928;
const CY: f64 = 185.2157;
// baseline
const BASELINE: f64 = 0.573;

// bilinear interpolation
fn get_pixel_value(img: &Luma<u8>, x: f64, y: f64) -> f64 {
    let mut x = x;
    let mut y = y;
    // boundary check
    if x < 0.0 {
        x = 0.0;
    }
    if y < 0.0 {
        y = 0.0;
    }
    if x >= img.width() as f64 {
        x = img.width() as f64 - 1.0;
    }
    if y >= img.height() as f64 {
        y = img.height() as f64 - 1.0;
    }
    let x_floor = x.floor() as u32;
    let y_floor = y.floor() as u32;
    let xx = x - x_floor as f64;
    let yy = y - y_floor as f64;
    let data = |x: u32, y: u32| img.get_pixel(x, y)[0] as f64;
    (1.0 - xx) * (1.0 - yy) * data(x_floor, y_floor) +
    xx * (1.0 - yy) * data(x_floor + 1, y_floor) +
    (1.0 - xx) * yy * data(x_floor, y_floor + 1) +
    xx * yy * data(x_floor + 1, y_floor + 1)
}

// 用于并行累加雅可比矩阵的结构体
struct JacobianAccumulator<'a> {
    img1: &'a Luma<u8>,
    img2: &'a Luma<u8>,
    px_ref: &'a Vec<Vector2<f64>>,
    depth_ref: &'a Vec<f64>,
    T21: &'a mut SE3<f64>,
    projection: Vec<Vector2<f64>>,
    H: Matrix6<f64>,
    b: Vector6<f64>,
    cost: f64,
}

impl<'a> JacobianAccumulator<'a> {
    fn new(
        img1: &'a Luma<u8>,
        img2: &'a Luma<u8>,
        px_ref: &'a Vec<Vector2<f64>>,
        depth_ref: &'a Vec<f64>,
        T21: &'a mut SE3<f64>
    ) -> Self {
        let projection = vec![Vector2::zeros(); px_ref.len()];
        Self {
            img1,
            img2,
            px_ref,
            depth_ref,
            T21,
            projection,
            H: Matrix6::zeros(),
            b: Vector6::zeros(),
            cost: 0.0,
        }
    }

    fn accumulate_jacobian(&mut self, range: std::ops::Range<usize>) {
        let half_patch_size = 1;
        let mut cnt_good = 0;
        let mut hessian = Matrix6::zeros();
        let mut bias = Vector6::zeros();
        let mut cost_tmp = 0.0;

        for i in range {
            // compute the projection in the second image
            let point_ref = self.depth_ref[i] * Vector3::new(
                (self.px_ref[i].x - CX) / FX,
                (self.px_ref[i].y - CY) / FY,
                1.0
            );
            let point_cur = self.T21.apply(point_ref.as_view());
            if point_cur[2] < 0.0 {   // depth invalid
                continue;
            }

            let u = FX * point_cur[0] / point_cur[2] + CX;
            let v = FY * point_cur[1] / point_cur[2] + CY;
            if u < half_patch_size as f64 || u > (self.img2.width() - half_patch_size) as f64 ||
                v < half_patch_size as f64 || v > (self.img2.height() - half_patch_size) as f64 {
                continue;
            }

            self.projection[i] = Vector2::new(u, v);
            let X = point_cur[0];
            let Y = point_cur[1];
            let Z = point_cur[2];
            let Z2 = Z * Z;
            let Z_inv = 1.0 / Z;
            let Z2_inv = Z_inv * Z_inv;
            cnt_good += 1;

            // and compute error and jacobian
            for x in -half_patch_size..=half_patch_size {
                for y in -half_patch_size..=half_patch_size {
                    let error = get_pixel_value(self.img1, self.px_ref[i].x + x as f64, self.px_ref[i].y + y as f64) -
                                get_pixel_value(self.img2, u + x as f64, v + y as f64);
                    let mut J_pixel_xi = Matrix2x6::zeros();
                    let mut J_img_pixel = Vector2::zeros();

                    J_pixel_xi[(0, 0)] = FX * Z_inv;
                    J_pixel_xi[(0, 1)] = 0.0;
                    J_pixel_xi[(0, 2)] = -FX * X * Z2_inv;
                    J_pixel_xi[(0, 3)] = -FX * X * Y * Z2_inv;
                    J_pixel_xi[(0, 4)] = FX + FX * X * X * Z2_inv;
                    J_pixel_xi[(0, 5)] = -FX * Y * Z_inv;

                    J_pixel_xi[(1, 0)] = 0.0;
                    J_pixel_xi[(1, 1)] = FY * Z_inv;
                    J_pixel_xi[(1, 2)] = -FY * Y * Z2_inv;
                    J_pixel_xi[(1, 3)] = -FY - FY * Y * Y * Z2_inv;
                    J_pixel_xi[(1, 4)] = FY * X * Y * Z2_inv;
                    J_pixel_xi[(1, 5)] = FY * X * Z_inv;

                    J_img_pixel = Vector2::new(
                        0.5 * (get_pixel_value(self.img2, u + 1.0 + x as f64, v + y as f64) - get_pixel_value(self.img2, u - 1.0 + x as f64, v + y as f64)),
                        0.5 * (get_pixel_value(self.img2, u + x as f64, v + 1.0 + y as f64) - get_pixel_value(self.img2, u + x as f64, v - 1.0 + y as f64))
                    );

                    // total jacobian
                    let J = -1.0 * (J_img_pixel.transpose() * J_pixel_xi).transpose();

                    hessian += J * J.transpose();
                    bias += -error * J;
                    cost_tmp += error * error;
                }
            }
        }

        if cnt_good > 0 {
            self.H += hessian;
            self.b += bias;
            self.cost += cost_tmp / cnt_good as f64;
        }
    }

    fn hessian(&self) -> Matrix6<f64> {
        self.H
    }

    fn bias(&self) -> Vector6<f64> {
        self.b
    }

    fn cost_func(&self) -> f64 {
        self.cost
    }

    fn projected_points(&self) -> &Vec<Vector2<f64>> {
        &self.projection
    }

    fn reset(&mut self) {
        self.H = Matrix6::zeros();
        self.b = Vector6::zeros();
        self.cost = 0.0;
    }
}

// 单层直接法位姿估计
fn direct_pose_estimation_single_layer(
    img1: &Luma<u8>,
    img2: &Luma<u8>,
    px_ref: &Vec<Vector2<f64>>,
    depth_ref: &Vec<f64>,
    T21: &mut SE3<f64>
) {
    let iterations = 10;
    let mut cost = 0.0;
    let mut last_cost = 0.0;
    let t1 = Instant::now();
    let mut jaco_accu = JacobianAccumulator::new(img1, img2, px_ref, depth_ref, T21);

    for iter in 0..iterations {
        jaco_accu.reset();
        jaco_accu.accumulate_jacobian(0..px_ref.len());
        let H = jaco_accu.hessian();
        let b = jaco_accu.bias();

        // solve update and put it into estimation
        let update = H.lu().solve(&b).unwrap_or_else(|_| {
            eprintln!("Matrix solve failed!");
            Vector6::zeros()
        });
        if update[0].is_nan() {
            // sometimes occurred when we have a black or white patch and H is irreversible
            eprintln!("update is nan");
            break;
        }
        *T21 = SE3::exp((&update).into()) * T21.clone();
        cost = jaco_accu.cost_func();

        if iter > 0 && cost > last_cost {
            eprintln!("cost increased: {}, {}", cost, last_cost);
            break;
        }
        if update.norm() < 1e-3 {
            // converge
            break;
        }

        last_cost = cost;
        println!("iteration: {}, cost: {}", iter, cost);
    }

    println!("T21 = \n{}", T21.to_matrix());
    let t2 = Instant::now();
    let time_used = t2.duration_since(t1).as_secs_f64();
    println!("direct method for single layer: {}", time_used);

    // plot the projected pixels here
    let mut img2_show = img2.to_rgb8();
    let projection = jaco_accu.projected_points();
    for i in 0..px_ref.len() {
        let p_ref = px_ref[i];
        let p_cur = projection[i];
        if p_cur.x > 0.0 && p_cur.y > 0.0 {
            draw_cross_mut(&mut img2_show, Rgb([0, 250, 0]), p_cur.x as i32, p_cur.y as i32);
            draw_line_segment_mut(&mut img2_show, (p_ref.x as i32, p_ref.y as i32), (p_cur.x as i32, p_cur.y as i32), Rgb([0, 250, 0]));
        }
    }
    img2_show.save("current.png").unwrap();
}

// 多层直接法位姿估计
fn direct_pose_estimation_multi_layer(
    img1: &Luma<u8>,
    img2: &Luma<u8>,
    px_ref: &Vec<Vector2<f64>>,
    depth_ref: &Vec<f64>,
    T21: &mut SE3<f64>
) {
    // parameters
    let pyramids = 4;
    let pyramid_scale = 0.5;
    let scales = [1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125];

    // create pyramids
    let mut pyr1 = Vec::new();
    let mut pyr2 = Vec::new();
    for i in 0..pyramids {
        if i == 0 {
            pyr1.push(img1.clone());
            pyr2.push(img2.clone());
        } else {
            let img1_pyr = image::imageops::resize(&pyr1[i - 1], (pyr1[i - 1].width() as f64 * pyramid_scale) as u32, (pyr1[i - 1].height() as f64 * pyramid_scale) as u32, image::imageops::FilterType::Nearest);
            let img2_pyr = image::imageops::resize(&pyr2[i - 1], (pyr2[i - 1].width() as f64 * pyramid_scale) as u32, (pyr2[i - 1].height() as f64 * pyramid_scale) as u32, image::imageops::FilterType::Nearest);
            pyr1.push(img1_pyr.to_luma8());
            pyr2.push(img2_pyr.to_luma8());
        }
    }

    let fxG = FX;
    let fyG = FY;
    let cxG = CX;
    let cyG = CY;  // backup the old values
    for level in (0..pyramids).rev() {
        let mut px_ref_pyr = Vec::new();
        for px in px_ref {
            px_ref_pyr.push(scales[level] * px);
        }

        // scale fx, fy, cx, cy in different pyramid levels
        let fx = fxG * scales[level];
        let fy = fyG * scales[level];
        let cx = cxG * scales[level];
        let cy = cyG * scales[level];
        direct_pose_estimation_single_layer(&pyr1[level], &pyr2[level], &px_ref_pyr, depth_ref, T21);
    }
}

fn main() {
    let left_file = "./left.png";
    let disparity_file = "./disparity.png";
    let fmt_others = "./%06d.png";

    let left_img = open(left_file).unwrap().to_luma8();
    let disparity_img = open(disparity_file).unwrap().to_luma8();

    // let's randomly pick pixels in the first image and generate some 3d points in the first image's frame
    let mut rng = rand::thread_rng();
    let nPoints = 2000;
    let boarder = 20;
    let mut pixels_ref = Vec::new();
    let mut depth_ref = Vec::new();

    // generate pixels in ref and load depth data
    for _ in 0..nPoints {
        let x = rng.gen_range(boarder..left_img.width() - boarder) as f64;
        let y = rng.gen_range(boarder..left_img.height() - boarder) as f64;
        let disparity = disparity_img.get_pixel(x as u32, y as u32)[0] as f64;
        let depth = FX * BASELINE / disparity; // you know this is disparity to depth
        depth_ref.push(depth);
        pixels_ref.push(Vector2::new(x, y));
    } // end for

    // estimates 01~05.png's pose using this information
    let mut T_cur_ref = SE3::identity();

    for i in 1..6 {
        let img_path = format!(fmt_others, i);
        let img = open(&img_path).unwrap().to_luma8();
        // try single layer by uncomment this line
        // direct_pose_estimation_single_layer(&left_img, &img, &pixels_ref, &depth_ref, &mut T_cur_ref);
        direct_pose_estimation_multi_layer(&left_img, &img, &pixels_ref, &depth_ref, &mut T_cur_ref);
    }

}
```

### 代码解释与使用说明
1. **整体功能**：此代码实现了基于直接法的多层位姿估计，借助随机选取的像素点和深度信息，对不同图像间的位姿变换进行估计。
2. **关键函数与结构体**：
    - `get_pixel_value`：对图像进行双线性插值，获取指定位置的像素值。
    - `JacobianAccumulator`：用于并行累加雅可比矩阵、海森矩阵、偏置和代价。
    - `direct_pose_estimation_single_layer`：单层直接法位姿估计，通过迭代更新位姿，直至收敛或达到最大迭代次数。
    - `direct_pose_estimation_multi_layer`：多层直接法位姿估计，构建图像金字塔，从粗到精进行位姿估计。
3. **使用方法**：
    - 确保所需的图像文件（`left.png`、`disparity.png`、`01.png` - `05.png`）存在于当前工作目录中。
    - 运行代码，它会随机选取像素点并生成深度信息，随后对不同图像间的位姿变换进行估计。
    - 最终会输出每层迭代的代价和最终的位姿变换矩阵，并且会把投影点绘制在当前图像上并保存为`current.png`。

### 注意事项
- 代码中假定图像文件格式为支持的格式（如PNG），若文件格式不同，需进行相应调整。
- 代码里的矩阵求解部分若失败，会输出错误信息并使用零向量替代，实际应用中可能需要更完善的错误处理。 

## 问题
代码:
```rs
#![allow(dead_code)]
#![allow(unused_variables)]
#![allow(unused_imports)]
#![allow(unused_mut)]
#![allow(unused_assignments)]
#![cfg_attr(not(debug_assertions), windows_subsystem = "windows")]
#![allow(rustdoc::missing_crate_level_docs)]
#![allow(unsafe_code)]
#![allow(clippy::undocumented_unsafe_blocks)]
#![allow(unused_must_use)]
#![allow(non_snake_case)]

// 线性代数
use nalgebra::{
    DMatrix, DVector, Matrix3, Vector3, 
    Vector2, Matrix6, Vector6, Matrix2x6, 
    SMatrix, SVector
};

// 图像处理
use image::{
    open, ImageBuffer, Rgb, DynamicImage, Luma
};
use imageproc::{
    drawing::draw_cross_mut, drawing::draw_line_segment_mut
};

// ORB角点检测
use bye_orb_rs::{
    orb, fast, common::Matchable
};

// 图优化
use factrs::{
    assign_symbols,
    core::{BetweenResidual, GaussNewton, Graph, Values},
    dtype, fac,
    linalg::{Const, ForwardProp, Numeric, NumericalDiff, VectorX, DiffResult, MatrixX},
    residuals::Residual1,
    traits::*,
    variables::{VectorVar2, SE2, VectorVar3, SE3, SO2, SO3, MatrixLieGroup},
    containers::Key,
    noise::{GaussianNoise}
};

// 随机数
use rand::{
    Rng, SeedableRng
};
use rand::distributions::{
    Distribution, Uniform
};

// 时间
use std::time::Instant;

// 定义符号变量
assign_symbols!(X: VectorVar3);
assign_symbols!(Y: SE2);
assign_symbols!(Z: SE3);

// 相机内参
const FX: f64 = 718.856;
const FY: f64 = 718.856;
const CX: f64 = 607.1928;
const CY: f64 = 185.2157;
// 基线
const BASELINE: f64 = 0.573;

// 双线性插值
fn get_pixel_value(img: &Luma<u8>, x: f64, y: f64) -> f64 {
    let mut x = x;
    let mut y = y;
    // 边界检查
    if x < 0.0 {
        x = 0.0;
    }
    if y < 0.0 {
        y = 0.0;
    }
    if x >= img.width() as f64 {
        x = img.width() as f64 - 1.0;
    }
    if y >= img.height() as f64 {
        y = img.height() as f64 - 1.0;
    }
    let x_floor = x.floor() as u32;
    let y_floor = y.floor() as u32;
    let xx = x - x_floor as f64;
    let yy = y - y_floor as f64;
    let data = |x: u32, y: u32| img.get_pixel(x, y)[0] as f64;
    (1.0 - xx) * (1.0 - yy) * data(x_floor, y_floor) +
    xx * (1.0 - yy) * data(x_floor + 1, y_floor) +
    (1.0 - xx) * yy * data(x_floor, y_floor + 1) +
    xx * yy * data(x_floor + 1, y_floor + 1)
}

// 用于并行累加雅可比矩阵的结构体
struct JacobianAccumulator<'a> {
    img1: &'a Luma<u8>,
    img2: &'a Luma<u8>,
    px_ref: &'a Vec<Vector2<f64>>,
    depth_ref: &'a Vec<f64>,
    T21: &'a mut SE3<f64>,
    projection: Vec<Vector2<f64>>,
    H: Matrix6<f64>,
    b: Vector6<f64>,
    cost: f64,
}

impl<'a> JacobianAccumulator<'a> {
    fn new(
        img1: &'a Luma<u8>,
        img2: &'a Luma<u8>,
        px_ref: &'a Vec<Vector2<f64>>,
        depth_ref: &'a Vec<f64>,
        T21: &'a mut SE3<f64>
    ) -> Self {
        let projection = vec![Vector2::zeros(); px_ref.len()];
        Self {
            img1,
            img2,
            px_ref,
            depth_ref,
            T21,
            projection,
            H: Matrix6::zeros(),
            b: Vector6::zeros(),
            cost: 0.0,
        }
    }

    fn accumulate_jacobian(&mut self, range: std::ops::Range<usize>) {
        let half_patch_size = 1;
        let mut cnt_good = 0;
        let mut hessian = Matrix6::zeros();
        let mut bias = Vector6::zeros();
        let mut cost_tmp = 0.0;

        for i in range {
            // 计算在第二张图像中的投影
            let point_ref = self.depth_ref[i] * Vector3::new(
                (self.px_ref[i].x - CX) / FX,
                (self.px_ref[i].y - CY) / FY,
                1.0
            );
            let point_cur = self.T21.apply(point_ref.as_view());
            if point_cur[2] < 0.0 {   // 深度无效
                continue;
            }

            let u = FX * point_cur[0] / point_cur[2] + CX;
            let v = FY * point_cur[1] / point_cur[2] + CY;
            if u < half_patch_size as f64 || u > (self.img2.width() - half_patch_size) as f64 ||
                v < half_patch_size as f64 || v > (self.img2.height() - half_patch_size) as f64 {
                continue;
            }

            self.projection[i] = Vector2::new(u, v);
            let X = point_cur[0];
            let Y = point_cur[1];
            let Z = point_cur[2];
            let Z2 = Z * Z;
            let Z_inv = 1.0 / Z;
            let Z2_inv = Z_inv * Z_inv;
            cnt_good += 1;

            // 计算误差和雅可比
            for x in -half_patch_size..=half_patch_size {
                for y in -half_patch_size..=half_patch_size {
                    let error = get_pixel_value(self.img1, self.px_ref[i].x + x as f64, self.px_ref[i].y + y as f64) -
                                get_pixel_value(self.img2, u + x as f64, v + y as f64);
                    let mut J_pixel_xi = Matrix2x6::zeros();
                    let mut J_img_pixel = Vector2::zeros();

                    J_pixel_xi[(0, 0)] = FX * Z_inv;
                    J_pixel_xi[(0, 1)] = 0.0;
                    J_pixel_xi[(0, 2)] = -FX * X * Z2_inv;
                    J_pixel_xi[(0, 3)] = -FX * X * Y * Z2_inv;
                    J_pixel_xi[(0, 4)] = FX + FX * X * X * Z2_inv;
                    J_pixel_xi[(0, 5)] = -FX * Y * Z_inv;

                    J_pixel_xi[(1, 0)] = 0.0;
                    J_pixel_xi[(1, 1)] = FY * Z_inv;
                    J_pixel_xi[(1, 2)] = -FY * Y * Z2_inv;
                    J_pixel_xi[(1, 3)] = -FY - FY * Y * Y * Z2_inv;
                    J_pixel_xi[(1, 4)] = FY * X * Y * Z2_inv;
                    J_pixel_xi[(1, 5)] = FY * X * Z_inv;

                    J_img_pixel = Vector2::new(
                        0.5 * (get_pixel_value(self.img2, u + 1.0 + x as f64, v + y as f64) - get_pixel_value(self.img2, u - 1.0 + x as f64, v + y as f64)),
                        0.5 * (get_pixel_value(self.img2, u + x as f64, v + 1.0 + y as f64) - get_pixel_value(self.img2, u + x as f64, v - 1.0 + y as f64))
                    );

                    // 总雅可比
                    let J = -1.0 * (J_img_pixel.transpose() * J_pixel_xi).transpose();

                    hessian += J * J.transpose();
                    bias += -error * J;
                    cost_tmp += error * error;
                }
            }
        }

        if cnt_good > 0 {
            self.H += hessian;
            self.b += bias;
            self.cost += cost_tmp / cnt_good as f64;
        }
    }

    fn hessian(&self) -> Matrix6<f64> {
        self.H
    }

    fn bias(&self) -> Vector6<f64> {
        self.b
    }

    fn cost_func(&self) -> f64 {
        self.cost
    }

    fn projected_points(&self) -> &Vec<Vector2<f64>> {
        &self.projection
    }

    fn reset(&mut self) {
        self.H = Matrix6::zeros();
        self.b = Vector6::zeros();
        self.cost = 0.0;
    }
}

// 单层直接法位姿估计
fn direct_pose_estimation_single_layer(
    img1: &Luma<u8>,
    img2: &Luma<u8>,
    px_ref: &Vec<Vector2<f64>>,
    depth_ref: &Vec<f64>,
    T21: &mut SE3<f64>
) {
    let iterations = 10;
    let mut cost = 0.0;
    let mut last_cost = 0.0;
    let t1 = Instant::now();
    let mut jaco_accu = JacobianAccumulator::new(img1, img2, px_ref, depth_ref, T21);

    for iter in 0..iterations {
        jaco_accu.reset();
        jaco_accu.accumulate_jacobian(0..px_ref.len());
        let H = jaco_accu.hessian();
        let b = jaco_accu.bias();

        // 求解更新并放入估计
        let update = H.lu().solve(&b).unwrap_or_else(|_| {
            eprintln!("矩阵求解失败!");
            Vector6::zeros()
        });
        if update[0].is_nan() {
            // 有时发生在我们有一个黑色或白色的补丁并且H是不可逆的
            eprintln!("更新为nan");
            break;
        }
        *T21 = SE3::exp((&update).into()) * T21.clone();
        cost = jaco_accu.cost_func();

        if iter > 0 && cost > last_cost {
            eprintln!("成本增加: {}, {}", cost, last_cost);
            break;
        }
        if update.norm() < 1e-3 {
            // 收敛
            break;
        }

        last_cost = cost;
        println!("迭代: {}, 成本: {}", iter, cost);
    }

    println!("T21 = \n{}", T21.to_matrix());
    let t2 = Instant::now();
    let time_used = t2.duration_since(t1).as_secs_f64();
    println!("单层直接法: {}", time_used);

    // 在此处绘制投影像素
    let mut img2_show = img2.to_rgb8();
    let projection = jaco_accu.projected_points();
    for i in 0..px_ref.len() {
        let p_ref = px_ref[i];
        let p_cur = projection[i];
        if p_cur.x > 0.0 && p_cur.y > 0.0 {
            draw_cross_mut(&mut img2_show, Rgb([0, 250, 0]), p_cur.x as i32, p_cur.y as i32);
            draw_line_segment_mut(&mut img2_show, (p_ref.x as i32, p_ref.y as i32), (p_cur.x as i32, p_cur.y as i32), Rgb([0, 250, 0]));
        }
    }
    img2_show.save("ch8-direct_method-current.png").unwrap();
}

// 多层直接法位姿估计
fn direct_pose_estimation_multi_layer(
    img1: &Luma<u8>,
    img2: &Luma<u8>,
    px_ref: &Vec<Vector2<f64>>,
    depth_ref: &Vec<f64>,
    T21: &mut SE3<f64>
) {
    // 参数
    let pyramids = 4;
    let pyramid_scale = 0.5;
    let scales = [1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125];

    // 创建金字塔
    let mut pyr1 = Vec::new();
    let mut pyr2 = Vec::new();
    for i in 0..pyramids {
        if i == 0 {
            pyr1.push(img1.clone());
            pyr2.push(img2.clone());
        } else {
            let img1_pyr = image::imageops::resize(&pyr1[i - 1], (pyr1[i - 1].width() as f64 * pyramid_scale) as u32, (pyr1[i - 1].height() as f64 * pyramid_scale) as u32, image::imageops::FilterType::Nearest);
            let img2_pyr = image::imageops::resize(&pyr2[i - 1], (pyr2[i - 1].width() as f64 * pyramid_scale) as u32, (pyr2[i - 1].height() as f64 * pyramid_scale) as u32, image::imageops::FilterType::Nearest);
            pyr1.push(img1_pyr.to_luma8());
            pyr2.push(img2_pyr.to_luma8());
        }
    }

    let fxG = FX;
    let fyG = FY;
    let cxG = CX;
    let cyG = CY;  // 备份旧值
    for level in (0..pyramids).rev() {
        let mut px_ref_pyr = Vec::new();
        for px in px_ref {
            px_ref_pyr.push(scales[level] * px);
        }

        // 在不同的金字塔层次中缩放 fx, fy, cx, cy
        let fx = fxG * scales[level];
        let fy = fyG * scales[level];
        let cx = cxG * scales[level];
        let cy = cyG * scales[level];
        direct_pose_estimation_single_layer(&pyr1[level], &pyr2[level], &px_ref_pyr, depth_ref, T21);
    }
}

fn main() {
    let left_file = "./assets/ch8-left.png";
    let disparity_file = "./assets/ch8-disparity.png";
    let fmt_others = "./assets/ch8-%06d.png";

    let left_img = open(left_file).unwrap().to_luma8();
    let disparity_img = open(disparity_file).unwrap().to_luma8();

    // 我们在第一张图像中随机选择像素并在第一张图像的帧中生成一些3D点
    let mut rng = rand::thread_rng();
    let nPoints = 2000;
    let boarder = 20;
    let mut pixels_ref = Vec::new();
    let mut depth_ref = Vec::new();

    // 在ref中生成像素并加载深度数据
    for _ in 0..nPoints {
        let x = rng.gen_range(boarder..left_img.width() - boarder) as f64;
        let y = rng.gen_range(boarder..left_img.height() - boarder) as f64;
        let disparity = disparity_img.get_pixel(x as u32, y as u32)[0] as f64;
        let depth = FX * BASELINE / disparity; // 你知道这是视差到深度
        depth_ref.push(depth);
        pixels_ref.push(Vector2::new(x, y));
    } // 结束for

    // 使用此信息估计01~05.png的姿态
    let mut T_cur_ref = SE3::identity();

    for i in 1..6 {
        let img_path = format!("{} {}",fmt_others, i);
        let img = open(&img_path).unwrap().to_luma8();
        // 通过取消注释此行尝试单层
        // direct_pose_estimation_single_layer(&left_img, &img, &pixels_ref, &depth_ref, &mut T_cur_ref);
        direct_pose_estimation_multi_layer(&left_img, &img, &pixels_ref, &depth_ref, &mut T_cur_ref);
    }

}

```
给出代码需要修改的地方;

### 回答
代码中存在多个错误，主要集中在以下几个方面：

1. **变量名冲突**：`X`, `Y`, `Z` 已经被 `assign_symbols!` 宏定义为符号变量，因此在代码中不能再用这些名字作为局部变量名。
2. **`Luma<u8>` 类型的方法缺失**：`Luma<u8>` 类型没有 `width`, `height`, `get_pixel`, `to_rgb8` 等方法。
3. **类型不匹配**：`draw_line_segment_mut` 函数期望的参数类型是 `f32`，但传入的是 `i32`。
4. **`resize` 函数的参数类型不匹配**：`resize` 函数期望的参数类型是实现了 `GenericImageView` trait 的类型，但传入的是 `Luma<u8>`。
5. **`unwrap_or_else` 闭包参数错误**：`unwrap_or_else` 期望的闭包是无参数的，但传入的闭包带有一个参数。

### 修改建议

1. **修改变量名冲突**：
   将 `X`, `Y`, `Z` 改为其他名字，例如 `x_val`, `y_val`, `z_val`。

   ```rust
   let x_val = point_cur[0];
   let y_val = point_cur[1];
   let z_val = point_cur[2];
   ```

2. **修复 `Luma<u8>` 类型的方法缺失**：
   使用 `ImageBuffer` 类型代替 `Luma<u8>`，因为 `ImageBuffer` 实现了 `GenericImageView` trait，提供了 `width`, `height`, `get_pixel` 等方法。

   ```rust
   use image::ImageBuffer;

   fn get_pixel_value(img: &ImageBuffer<Luma<u8>, Vec<u8>>, x: f64, y: f64) -> f64 {
       // ...
   }

   struct JacobianAccumulator<'a> {
       img1: &'a ImageBuffer<Luma<u8>, Vec<u8>>,
       img2: &'a ImageBuffer<Luma<u8>, Vec<u8>>,
       // ...
   }
   ```

3. **修复 `draw_line_segment_mut` 参数类型不匹配**：
   将 `i32` 转换为 `f32`。

   ```rust
   draw_line_segment_mut(
       &mut img2_show,
       (p_ref.x as f32, p_ref.y as f32),
       (p_cur.x as f32, p_cur.y as f32),
       Rgb([0, 250, 0]),
   );
   ```

4. **修复 `resize` 函数的参数类型不匹配**：
   使用 `ImageBuffer` 类型代替 `Luma<u8>`。

   ```rust
   let img1_pyr = image::imageops::resize(
       &pyr1[i - 1],
       (pyr1[i - 1].width() as f64 * pyramid_scale) as u32,
       (pyr1[i - 1].height() as f64 * pyramid_scale) as u32,
       image::imageops::FilterType::Nearest,
   );
   ```

5. **修复 `unwrap_or_else` 闭包参数错误**：
   将闭包改为无参数形式。

   ```rust
   let update = H.lu().solve(&b).unwrap_or_else(|| {
       eprintln!("矩阵求解失败!");
       Vector6::zeros()
   });
   ```

### 修改后的代码片段

```rust
// 修改后的 get_pixel_value 函数
fn get_pixel_value(img: &ImageBuffer<Luma<u8>, Vec<u8>>, x: f64, y: f64) -> f64 {
    let mut x = x;
    let mut y = y;
    // 边界检查
    if x < 0.0 {
        x = 0.0;
    }
    if y < 0.0 {
        y = 0.0;
    }
    if x >= img.width() as f64 {
        x = img.width() as f64 - 1.0;
    }
    if y >= img.height() as f64 {
        y = img.height() as f64 - 1.0;
    }
    let x_floor = x.floor() as u32;
    let y_floor = y.floor() as u32;
    let xx = x - x_floor as f64;
    let yy = y - y_floor as f64;
    let data = |x: u32, y: u32| img.get_pixel(x, y)[0] as f64;
    (1.0 - xx) * (1.0 - yy) * data(x_floor, y_floor) +
    xx * (1.0 - yy) * data(x_floor + 1, y_floor) +
    (1.0 - xx) * yy * data(x_floor, y_floor + 1) +
    xx * yy * data(x_floor + 1, y_floor + 1)
}

// 修改后的 JacobianAccumulator 结构体
struct JacobianAccumulator<'a> {
    img1: &'a ImageBuffer<Luma<u8>, Vec<u8>>,
    img2: &'a ImageBuffer<Luma<u8>, Vec<u8>>,
    px_ref: &'a Vec<Vector2<f64>>,
    depth_ref: &'a Vec<f64>,
    T21: &'a mut SE3<f64>,
    projection: Vec<Vector2<f64>>,
    H: Matrix6<f64>,
    b: Vector6<f64>,
    cost: f64,
}

// 修改后的 direct_pose_estimation_single_layer 函数
fn direct_pose_estimation_single_layer(
    img1: &ImageBuffer<Luma<u8>, Vec<u8>>,
    img2: &ImageBuffer<Luma<u8>, Vec<u8>>,
    px_ref: &Vec<Vector2<f64>>,
    depth_ref: &Vec<f64>,
    T21: &mut SE3<f64>
) {
    // ...
    let mut img2_show = img2.to_rgb();
    let projection = jaco_accu.projected_points();
    for i in 0..px_ref.len() {
        let p_ref = px_ref[i];
        let p_cur = projection[i];
        if p_cur.x > 0.0 && p_cur.y > 0.0 {
            draw_cross_mut(&mut img2_show, Rgb([0, 250, 0]), p_cur.x as i32, p_cur.y as i32);
            draw_line_segment_mut(
                &mut img2_show,
                (p_ref.x as f32, p_ref.y as f32),
                (p_cur.x as f32, p_cur.y as f32),
                Rgb([0, 250, 0]),
            );
        }
    }
    img2_show.save("ch8-direct_method-current.png").unwrap();
}

// 修改后的 direct_pose_estimation_multi_layer 函数
fn direct_pose_estimation_multi_layer(
    img1: &ImageBuffer<Luma<u8>, Vec<u8>>,
    img2: &ImageBuffer<Luma<u8>, Vec<u8>>,
    px_ref: &Vec<Vector2<f64>>,
    depth_ref: &Vec<f64>,
    T21: &mut SE3<f64>
) {
    // ...
    let mut pyr1 = Vec::new();
    let mut pyr2 = Vec::new();
    for i in 0..pyramids {
        if i == 0 {
            pyr1.push(img1.clone());
            pyr2.push(img2.clone());
        } else {
            let img1_pyr = image::imageops::resize(
                &pyr1[i - 1],
                (pyr1[i - 1].width() as f64 * pyramid_scale) as u32,
                (pyr1[i - 1].height() as f64 * pyramid_scale) as u32,
                image::imageops::FilterType::Nearest,
            );
            let img2_pyr = image::imageops::resize(
                &pyr2[i - 1],
                (pyr2[i - 1].width() as f64 * pyramid_scale) as u32,
                (pyr2[i - 1].height() as f64 * pyramid_scale) as u32,
                image::imageops::FilterType::Nearest,
            );
            pyr1.push(img1_pyr);
            pyr2.push(img2_pyr);
        }
    }
    // ...
}
```

### 其他注意事项

- **`to_rgb8` 方法**：`Luma<u8>` 没有 `to_rgb8` 方法，但 `ImageBuffer` 有 `to_rgb` 方法，可以使用 `to_rgb` 代替。
- **`unwrap_or_else` 闭包**：确保闭包是无参数的。

通过这些修改，代码应该能够成功编译并运行。


## 问题
```rs
fn accumulate_jacobian(&mut self, range: std::ops::Range<usize>) {
        let half_patch_size = 1;
        let mut cnt_good = 0;
        let mut hessian = Matrix6::zeros();
        let mut bias = Vector6::zeros();
        let mut cost_tmp = 0.0;

        for i in range {
            // 计算在第二张图像中的投影
            let point_ref = self.depth_ref[i] * Vector3::new(
                (self.px_ref[i].x - CX) / FX,
                (self.px_ref[i].y - CY) / FY,
                1.0
            );
            let point_cur = self.T21.apply(point_ref.as_view());
            if point_cur[2] < 0.0 {   // 深度无效
                continue;
            }

            let u = FX * point_cur[0] / point_cur[2] + CX;
            let v = FY * point_cur[1] / point_cur[2] + CY;
            if u < half_patch_size as f64 || u > (self.img2.width() - half_patch_size) as f64 ||
                v < half_patch_size as f64 || v > (self.img2.height() - half_patch_size) as f64 {
                continue;
            }

            self.projection[i] = Vector2::new(u, v);
            let X = point_cur[0];
            let Y = point_cur[1];
            let Z = point_cur[2];
            let Z2 = Z * Z;
            let Z_inv = 1.0 / Z;
            let Z2_inv = Z_inv * Z_inv;
            cnt_good += 1;

            // 计算误差和雅可比
            for x in -half_patch_size..=half_patch_size {
                for y in -half_patch_size..=half_patch_size {
                    let error = get_pixel_value(self.img1, self.px_ref[i].x + x as f64, self.px_ref[i].y + y as f64) -
                                get_pixel_value(self.img2, u + x as f64, v + y as f64);
                    let mut J_pixel_xi = Matrix2x6::zeros();
                    let mut J_img_pixel = Vector2::zeros();

                    J_pixel_xi[(0, 0)] = FX * Z_inv;
                    J_pixel_xi[(0, 1)] = 0.0;
                    J_pixel_xi[(0, 2)] = -FX * X * Z2_inv;
                    J_pixel_xi[(0, 3)] = -FX * X * Y * Z2_inv;
                    J_pixel_xi[(0, 4)] = FX + FX * X * X * Z2_inv;
                    J_pixel_xi[(0, 5)] = -FX * Y * Z_inv;

                    J_pixel_xi[(1, 0)] = 0.0;
                    J_pixel_xi[(1, 1)] = FY * Z_inv;
                    J_pixel_xi[(1, 2)] = -FY * Y * Z2_inv;
                    J_pixel_xi[(1, 3)] = -FY - FY * Y * Y * Z2_inv;
                    J_pixel_xi[(1, 4)] = FY * X * Y * Z2_inv;
                    J_pixel_xi[(1, 5)] = FY * X * Z_inv;

                    J_img_pixel = Vector2::new(
                        0.5 * (get_pixel_value(self.img2, u + 1.0 + x as f64, v + y as f64) - get_pixel_value(self.img2, u - 1.0 + x as f64, v + y as f64)),
                        0.5 * (get_pixel_value(self.img2, u + x as f64, v + 1.0 + y as f64) - get_pixel_value(self.img2, u + x as f64, v - 1.0 + y as f64))
                    );

                    // 总雅可比
                    let J = -1.0 * (J_img_pixel.transpose() * J_pixel_xi).transpose();

                    hessian += J * J.transpose();
                    bias += -error * J;
                    cost_tmp += error * error;
                }
            }
        }

        if cnt_good > 0 {
            self.H += hessian;
            self.b += bias;
            self.cost += cost_tmp / cnt_good as f64;
        }
    }
```
报错:
```sh
error[E0600]: cannot apply unary operator `-` to type `u32`
   --> examples/ch8-direct_method.rs:169:22
    |
169 |             for x in -half_patch_size..=half_patch_size {
    |                      ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^ cannot apply unary operator `-`
    |
    = note: unsigned values cannot be negated

error[E0600]: cannot apply unary operator `-` to type `u32`
   --> examples/ch8-direct_method.rs:170:26
    |
170 |                 for y in -half_patch_size..=half_patch_size {
    |                          ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^ cannot apply unary operator `-`
    |
    = note: unsigned values cannot be negated
```
给出修正方式;

### 回答
错误的原因在于 `half_patch_size` 是一个 `u32` 类型的变量，而 `u32` 是无符号整数类型，不能直接应用一元负号运算符 `-`。为了修正这个错误，你需要将 `half_patch_size` 转换为有符号整数类型（如 `i32`），然后再进行取负操作。

你可以通过以下两种方式来修正这个错误：

### 方法 1: 将 `half_patch_size` 声明为 `i32` 类型
直接将 `half_patch_size` 声明为 `i32` 类型，这样你就可以直接对它进行取负操作。

```rust
let half_patch_size: i32 = 1;  // 将 half_patch_size 声明为 i32 类型
```

### 方法 2: 在取负时进行类型转换
如果你希望保持 `half_patch_size` 为 `u32` 类型，可以在取负时将其转换为 `i32` 类型。

```rust
for x in -(half_patch_size as i32)..=half_patch_size as i32 {
    for y in -(half_patch_size as i32)..=half_patch_size as i32 {
        // 其他代码保持不变
    }
}
```

### 修正后的代码
以下是使用方法 1 修正后的代码片段：

```rust
fn accumulate_jacobian(&mut self, range: std::ops::Range<usize>) {
    let half_patch_size: i32 = 1;  // 将 half_patch_size 声明为 i32 类型
    let mut cnt_good = 0;
    let mut hessian = Matrix6::zeros();
    let mut bias = Vector6::zeros();
    let mut cost_tmp = 0.0;

    for i in range {
        // 计算在第二张图像中的投影
        let point_ref = self.depth_ref[i] * Vector3::new(
            (self.px_ref[i].x - CX) / FX,
            (self.px_ref[i].y - CY) / FY,
            1.0
        );
        let point_cur = self.T21.apply(point_ref.as_view());
        if point_cur[2] < 0.0 {   // 深度无效
            continue;
        }

        let u = FX * point_cur[0] / point_cur[2] + CX;
        let v = FY * point_cur[1] / point_cur[2] + CY;
        if u < half_patch_size as f64 || u > (self.img2.width() - half_patch_size) as f64 ||
            v < half_patch_size as f64 || v > (self.img2.height() - half_patch_size) as f64 {
            continue;
        }

        self.projection[i] = Vector2::new(u, v);
        let X = point_cur[0];
        let Y = point_cur[1];
        let Z = point_cur[2];
        let Z2 = Z * Z;
        let Z_inv = 1.0 / Z;
        let Z2_inv = Z_inv * Z_inv;
        cnt_good += 1;

        // 计算误差和雅可比
        for x in -half_patch_size..=half_patch_size {
            for y in -half_patch_size..=half_patch_size {
                let error = get_pixel_value(self.img1, self.px_ref[i].x + x as f64, self.px_ref[i].y + y as f64) -
                            get_pixel_value(self.img2, u + x as f64, v + y as f64);
                let mut J_pixel_xi = Matrix2x6::zeros();
                let mut J_img_pixel = Vector2::zeros();

                J_pixel_xi[(0, 0)] = FX * Z_inv;
                J_pixel_xi[(0, 1)] = 0.0;
                J_pixel_xi[(0, 2)] = -FX * X * Z2_inv;
                J_pixel_xi[(0, 3)] = -FX * X * Y * Z2_inv;
                J_pixel_xi[(0, 4)] = FX + FX * X * X * Z2_inv;
                J_pixel_xi[(0, 5)] = -FX * Y * Z_inv;

                J_pixel_xi[(1, 0)] = 0.0;
                J_pixel_xi[(1, 1)] = FY * Z_inv;
                J_pixel_xi[(1, 2)] = -FY * Y * Z2_inv;
                J_pixel_xi[(1, 3)] = -FY - FY * Y * Y * Z2_inv;
                J_pixel_xi[(1, 4)] = FY * X * Y * Z2_inv;
                J_pixel_xi[(1, 5)] = FY * X * Z_inv;

                J_img_pixel = Vector2::new(
                    0.5 * (get_pixel_value(self.img2, u + 1.0 + x as f64, v + y as f64) - get_pixel_value(self.img2, u - 1.0 + x as f64, v + y as f64)),
                    0.5 * (get_pixel_value(self.img2, u + x as f64, v + 1.0 + y as f64) - get_pixel_value(self.img2, u + x as f64, v - 1.0 + y as f64))
                );

                // 总雅可比
                let J = -1.0 * (J_img_pixel.transpose() * J_pixel_xi).transpose();

                hessian += J * J.transpose();
                bias += -error * J;
                cost_tmp += error * error;
            }
        }
    }

    if cnt_good > 0 {
        self.H += hessian;
        self.b += bias;
        self.cost += cost_tmp / cnt_good as f64;
    }
}
```

这样修改后，代码应该可以正常编译并运行。

## 问题
提示:[https://docs.rs/image/0.25.5/image/struct.ImageBuffer.html]
代码:
```rs
// 在此处绘制投影像素
let mut img2_show = img2.to_rgb8();

// 转换图片为灰度图
pyr1.push(img1_pyr.to_luma8());
pyr2.push(img2_pyr.to_luma8());
```
报错:
```sh
error[E0599]: no method named `to_rgb8` found for reference `&ImageBuffer<Luma<u8>, Vec<u8>>` in the current scope
   --> examples/ch8-direct_method.rs:287:30
    |
287 |     let mut img2_show = img2.to_rgb8();
    |                              ^^^^^^^ method not found in `&ImageBuffer<Luma<u8>, Vec<u8>>`

error[E0599]: no method named `to_luma8` found for struct `ImageBuffer<Luma<u8>, Vec<u8>>` in the current scope
   --> examples/ch8-direct_method.rs:323:32
    |
323 |             pyr1.push(img1_pyr.to_luma8());
    |                                ^^^^^^^^ method not found in `ImageBuffer<Luma<u8>, Vec<u8>>`

error[E0599]: no method named `to_luma8` found for struct `ImageBuffer<Luma<u8>, Vec<u8>>` in the current scope
   --> examples/ch8-direct_method.rs:324:32
    |
324 |             pyr2.push(img2_pyr.to_luma8());
    |                                ^^^^^^^^ method not found in `ImageBuffer<Luma<u8>, Vec<u8>>`

For more information about this error, try `rustc --explain E0599`.
error: could not compile `exp65-rust-ziglang-slambook2` (example "ch8-direct_method") due to 3 previous errors
```
根据提示修正代码报错;

### 回答
### 错误点分析
1. **`img2.to_rgb8()`**: `img2`是`&ImageBuffer<Luma<u8>, Vec<u8>>`类型的引用，`to_rgb8`方法并非为引用类型定义的，所以无法调用。
2. **`img1_pyr.to_luma8()`和`img2_pyr.to_luma8()`**: `img1_pyr`和`img2_pyr`属于`ImageBuffer<Luma<u8>, Vec<u8>>`类型，该类型已经是灰度图像，因此不存在`to_luma8`方法。

### 优化点
1. 调用方法前要去除引用。
2. 若图像已是灰度图，就无需再调用`to_luma8`方法。

### 修改后的代码
```rs
// 在此处绘制投影像素
// 去除引用
let mut img2_show = (*img2).to_rgb8();

// 转换图片为灰度图
// 图片已是灰度图，直接使用
pyr1.push(img1_pyr);
pyr2.push(img2_pyr);
```
此修改去除了不必要的方法调用，避免了编译错误。

根据你提供的错误信息和文档，问题出在你试图在 `ImageBuffer<Luma<u8>, Vec<u8>>` 类型上调用 `to_rgb8` 和 `to_luma8` 方法，但这些方法并不存在于该类型上。为了解决这个问题，你可以使用 `DynamicImage` 作为中间类型来进行颜色模式的转换。
以下是修正后的代码：
```rs
// 转换图片为灰度图
let img1_pyr DynamicImage::ImageLuma8(img1_pyr.to_luma8());
let img2_pyr DynamicImage::ImageLuma8(img2_pyr.to_luma8());
// 在此处绘制投影像素
let mut img2_show = img2_pyr.to_rgb8();
```
具体步骤如下：
1. **转换为灰度图**：
   - 使用 `DynamicImage::ImageLuma8(img1_pyr.to_luma8())` 将 `img1_pyr` 转换为灰度图。
   - 使用 `DynamicImage::ImageLuma8(img2_pyr.to_luma8())` 将 `img2_pyr` 转换为灰度图。
2. **转换为RGB图**：
   - 使用 `img2_pyr.to_rgb8()` 将 `img2_pyr` 转换为RGB图。
这样，你就可以正确地进行颜色模式的转换而不会遇到方法不存在的问题。参考文档中的相关示例和说明。

# 问题
总结网页内容:[https://github.com/image-rs/image/blob/a7af546c292c0933986772adeba077e56947181e/src/buffer.rs#L656]

提示:
```rs
//! Contains the generic `ImageBuffer` struct.
use num_traits::Zero;
use std::fmt;
use std::marker::PhantomData;
use std::ops::{Deref, DerefMut, Index, IndexMut, Range};
use std::path::Path;
use std::slice::{ChunksExact, ChunksExactMut};

use crate::color::{FromColor, Luma, LumaA, Rgb, Rgba};
use crate::dynimage::{save_buffer, save_buffer_with_format, write_buffer_with_format};
use crate::error::ImageResult;
use crate::flat::{FlatSamples, SampleLayout};
use crate::image::{GenericImage, GenericImageView, ImageEncoder, ImageFormat};
use crate::math::Rect;
use crate::traits::{EncodableLayout, Pixel, PixelWithColorType};
use crate::utils::expand_packed;
use crate::DynamicImage;

/// Iterate over pixel refs.
pub struct Pixels<'a, P: Pixel + 'a>
where
    P::Subpixel: 'a,
{
    chunks: ChunksExact<'a, P::Subpixel>,
}

impl<'a, P: Pixel + 'a> Iterator for Pixels<'a, P>
where
    P::Subpixel: 'a,
{
    type Item = &'a P;

    #[inline(always)]
    fn next(&mut self) -> Option<&'a P> {
        self.chunks.next().map(|v| <P as Pixel>::from_slice(v))
    }

    #[inline(always)]
    fn size_hint(&self) -> (usize, Option<usize>) {
        let len = self.len();
        (len, Some(len))
    }
}

impl<'a, P: Pixel + 'a> ExactSizeIterator for Pixels<'a, P>
where
    P::Subpixel: 'a,
{
    fn len(&self) -> usize {
        self.chunks.len()
    }
}

impl<'a, P: Pixel + 'a> DoubleEndedIterator for Pixels<'a, P>
where
    P::Subpixel: 'a,
{
    #[inline(always)]
    fn next_back(&mut self) -> Option<&'a P> {
        self.chunks.next_back().map(|v| <P as Pixel>::from_slice(v))
    }
}

impl<P: Pixel> Clone for Pixels<'_, P> {
    fn clone(&self) -> Self {
        Pixels {
            chunks: self.chunks.clone(),
        }
    }
}

impl<P: Pixel> fmt::Debug for Pixels<'_, P>
where
    P::Subpixel: fmt::Debug,
{
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter) -> fmt::Result {
        f.debug_struct("Pixels")
            .field("chunks", &self.chunks)
            .finish()
    }
}

}

            .field("chunks", &self.chunks)
            .finish()

}

/// Iterate over rows of an image
///
/// This iterator is created with [`ImageBuffer::rows`]. See its document for details.
///
/// [`ImageBuffer::rows`]: ../struct.ImageBuffer.html#method.rows
pub struct Rows<'a, P: Pixel + 'a>
where
    <P as Pixel>::Subpixel: 'a,
{
    pixels: ChunksExact<'a, P::Subpixel>,
}

impl<'a, P: Pixel + 'a> Rows<'a, P> {
    /// Construct the iterator from image pixels. This is not public since it has a (hidden) panic
    /// condition. The `pixels` slice must be large enough so that all pixels are addressable.
    fn with_image(pixels: &'a [P::Subpixel], width: u32, height: u32) -> Self {
        let row_len = (width as usize) * usize::from(<P as Pixel>::CHANNEL_COUNT);
        if row_len == 0 {
            Rows {
                pixels: [].chunks_exact(1),
            }
        } else {
            let pixels = pixels
                .get(..row_len * height as usize)
                .expect("Pixel buffer has too few subpixels");
            // Rows are physically present. In particular, height is smaller than `usize::MAX` as
            // all subpixels can be indexed.
            Rows {
                pixels: pixels.chunks_exact(row_len),
            }
        }
    }
}

impl<'a, P: Pixel + 'a> Iterator for Rows<'a, P>
where
    P::Subpixel: 'a,
{
    type Item = Pixels<'a, P>;

    #[inline(always)]
    fn next(&mut self) -> Option<Pixels<'a, P>> {
        let row = self.pixels.next()?;
        Some(Pixels {
            // Note: this is not reached when CHANNEL_COUNT is 0.
            chunks: row.chunks_exact(<P as Pixel>::CHANNEL_COUNT as usize),
        })
    }

    #[inline(always)]
    fn size_hint(&self) -> (usize, Option<usize>) {
        let len = self.len();
        (len, Some(len))
    }
}

impl<'a, P: Pixel + 'a> ExactSizeIterator for Rows<'a, P>
where
    P::Subpixel: 'a,
{
    fn len(&self) -> usize {
        self.pixels.len()
    }
}

impl<'a, P: Pixel + 'a> DoubleEndedIterator for Rows<'a, P>
where
    P::Subpixel: 'a,
{
    #[inline(always)]
    fn next_back(&mut self) -> Option<Pixels<'a, P>> {
        let row = self.pixels.next_back()?;
        Some(Pixels {
            // Note: this is not reached when CHANNEL_COUNT is 0.
            chunks: row.chunks_exact(<P as Pixel>::CHANNEL_COUNT as usize),
        })
    }
}

impl<P: Pixel> Clone for Rows<'_, P> {
    fn clone(&self) -> Self {
        Rows {
            pixels: self.pixels.clone(),
        }
    }
}

impl<P: Pixel> fmt::Debug for Rows<'_, P>
where
    P::Subpixel: fmt::Debug,
{
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter) -> fmt::Result {
        f.debug_struct("Rows")
            .field("pixels", &self.pixels)
            .finish()
    }
}

/// Enumerate the pixels of an image.
pub struct EnumeratePixels<'a, P: Pixel + 'a>
where
    <P as Pixel>::Subpixel: 'a,
{
    pixels: Pixels<'a, P>,
    x: u32,
    y: u32,
    width: u32,
}

impl<'a, P: Pixel + 'a> Iterator for EnumeratePixels<'a, P>
where
    P::Subpixel: 'a,
{
    type Item = (u32, u32, &'a P);

    #[inline(always)]
    fn next(&mut self) -> Option<(u32, u32, &'a P)> {
        if self.x >= self.width {
            self.x = 0;
            self.y += 1;
        }
        let (x, y) = (self.x, self.y);
        self.x += 1;
        self.pixels.next().map(|p| (x, y, p))
    }

    #[inline(always)]
    fn size_hint(&self) -> (usize, Option<usize>) {
        let len = self.len();
        (len, Some(len))
    }
}

impl<'a, P: Pixel + 'a> ExactSizeIterator for EnumeratePixels<'a, P>
where
    P::Subpixel: 'a,
{
    fn len(&self) -> usize {
        self.pixels.len()
    }
}

impl<P: Pixel> Clone for EnumeratePixels<'_, P> {
    fn clone(&self) -> Self {
        EnumeratePixels {
            pixels: self.pixels.clone(),
            ..*self
        }
    }
}

impl<P: Pixel> fmt::Debug for EnumeratePixels<'_, P>
where
    P::Subpixel: fmt::Debug,
{
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter) -> fmt::Result {
        f.debug_struct("EnumeratePixels")
            .field("pixels", &self.pixels)
            .field("x", &self.x)
            .field("y", &self.y)
            .field("width", &self.width)
            .finish()
    }
}
